Detailed proof:
For a presheaf G on C, define G^+(c)=colim_{R∈J(c)} Hom(R,G). The canonical map G(c)→G^+(c) sends s to the matching family R↦s|_R. If id∈R for every covering R, then Hom(R,G)=G(c) via restriction along id. Indeed, a matching family (t_f)_{f∈R} is determined by t_id; conversely any t∈G(c) gives t_f=G(f)(t). Compatibility holds. Thus the transition maps in the colimit are identities, so G^+(c)=G(c). Applying plus twice yields sheafification aG, so (aG)(c)=G(c). (For abelian presheaves, one plus might suffice for separated?
